- **strace-syscall-diagnosis**: 基于 strace/ltrace 的系统调用级故障诊断技能（双轨：调用轨迹 + 内核语义）。当用户提到 strace、ltrace、 系统调用、syscall、EACCES、ENOENT、ENOMEM、EAGAIN、进程卡住、futex 阻塞、epoll 超时、 read 卡顿、库函数追踪等关键词时，必须使用本技能。覆盖场景：syscall 错误码模式识别（EACCES/ENOENT/ EAGAIN/ENOMEM）、慢 syscall 定位（futex/epoll_wait/read 阻塞）、库函数调用链异常追踪、 进程 hang/blocked 在 syscall、文件描述符泄漏、资源泄漏模式等系统调用级故障。 即使用户只说"进程卡住了/系统调用很慢/函数调用异常"也要触发本技能。 支持内核源码级根因分析（有源码时必须走双轨并行并交叉验证）。 ([openeuler-mirror/witty-diagnosis-agent](https://github.com/openeuler-mirror/witty-diagnosis-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/strace-syscall-diagnosis) · ★5)

([play14team/play14](https://github.com/play14team/play14/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/strapi-permissions-audit))

- **strat-assumption-audit**: 計画・予測・推奨の根拠となる前提を検証したい場面で使用する。明示・暗黙を問わず仮説を洗い出し、論拠の妥当性と脆弱性を評価した前提レジスターを生成する。 ([Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public](https://github.com/Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public/tree/HEAD/skills/strat-assumption-audit))
- **strat-business-case**: 投資の可否を経済的根拠で裏付けたいときに使う。コスト・便益・ROI・回収期間・感度分析を整理し、採算の見通しと投資対効果を示すビジネスケースを作成する。pptx スキルの roi_excel と組み合わせて使う。 ([Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public](https://github.com/Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public/tree/HEAD/skills/strat-business-case))
- **strat-decision-memo**: 決定権者が結論を先に読む1枚の意思決定メモを必要とするときに使用します。検討した選択肢・根拠・リスクを簡潔にまとめ、提言と決裁に必要なアクションを明示します。 ([Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public](https://github.com/Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public/tree/HEAD/skills/strat-decision-memo))

- **strat-growth-barriers**: 成長が停滞している、または目標を継続的に下回っているときに使用します。収益方程式を起点に成長の阻害要因とボトルネックを体系的に診断し、根本原因を優先順位付きで特定します。 ([Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public](https://github.com/Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public/tree/HEAD/skills/strat-growth-barriers))
- **strat-init**: Strat session init — tmonkey, fronta, pl_stats, failed tasky, t002 dohled, daily briefing. Trigger: 'init' v strat instanci. ([LG13-21/lg13-skills](https://github.com/LG13-21/lg13-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/lg13-skills/skills/strat-init))
- **strat-initiative-prioritizer**: 施策の候補が実行キャパシティを超えている場合に使用します。インパクト・工数・戦略適合度・依存関係を軸にスコアリングし、説明責任を果たせる優先順位付きのロードマップを作成します。 ([Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public](https://github.com/Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public/tree/HEAD/skills/strat-initiative-prioritizer))
- **strat-narrative**: 分析の知見を経営層向けのナラティブとして構造化する場面で使用する。ひとつの主メッセージを核にストーリーの流れを設計し、意思決定を促すエグゼクティブ・ナラティブを生成する。 ([Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public](https://github.com/Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public/tree/HEAD/skills/strat-narrative))
- **strat-operating-model**: 戦略の方向性が固まり、それを実行するためのオペレーティングモデルを設計したい場面で使用します。必要なケイパビリティの特定から、組織構造・業務プロセス・ガバナンス・人材・技術の各次元を設計し、現状とのギャップを明確にします。 ([Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public](https://github.com/Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public/tree/HEAD/skills/strat-operating-model))
- **strat-portfolio-review**: 複数の事業・製品・市場から成るポートフォリオを対象に、事業評価と資源配分の優先付けが必要な場面でお使いください。各ユニットの魅力度と競争優位を軸に Grow / Hold / Fix / Exit の方針を導き、選択と集中の意思決定を構造的に支援します。 ([Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public](https://github.com/Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public/tree/HEAD/skills/strat-portfolio-review))
- **strat-pricing**: 価格の設定・見直し、またはプライシングモデルの選定を行う場面で使用する。コスト積み上げ・バリューベース・競合基準の3方式を比較し、顧客の支払意欲とマージン・販売量のバランスを検討する。価格戦略の推奨案を成果物として生成する。 ([Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public](https://github.com/Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public/tree/HEAD/skills/strat-pricing))

- **strat-situation-assessment**: 市場・事業・オペレーション・組織の現状把握と診断を構造化したいときに使う。戦略的な意思決定の前に現状分析を行い、チーム全員が共有できる事実ベースのアセスメントを整理する。 ([Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public](https://github.com/Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public/tree/HEAD/skills/strat-situation-assessment))
- **strat-stakeholder-alignment**: 意思決定や変革推進において、ステークホルダーの影響力と関心を可視化し、関係者との合意形成をはかりたいとき、または関係者の巻き込みを設計したいときに使う。ステークホルダー調整計画を成果物として作成する。 ([Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public](https://github.com/Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public/tree/HEAD/skills/strat-stakeholder-alignment))
- **strat-strategic-options**: 戦略的な意思決定が未確定で、複数の選択肢を比較・評価する必要がある場面で使用します。明示的な評価基準に基づきオプションを構造化し、最善の選択肢を特定する意思決定分析を生成します。 ([Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public](https://github.com/Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public/tree/HEAD/skills/strat-strategic-options))
- **strat-transformation-roadmap**: 変革プログラムをフェーズに分けてロードマップ化し、ワークストリーム・マイルストーン・依存関係・早期成果を整理する場面で使用します。変革の方向性が定まり、実行計画を具体化する段階に入ったときに適用してください。 ([Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public](https://github.com/Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public/tree/HEAD/skills/strat-transformation-roadmap))

- **strat/blue-ocean**: Blue Ocean Strategy analysis (Kim & Mauborgne): Strategy Canvas mapping your offering vs competitors, ERRC Grid (Eliminate/Reduce/Raise/Create), Six Paths exploration, and a value innovation statement with copyability risk assessment. TRIGGER: "blue ocean", "oceano azul", "uncontested market space", "espaço de mercado por explorar", "ERRC", "strategy canvas", "/strat blue-ocean". SKIP: rating industry attractiveness and competitive forces -&gt; strat/five-forces (analyses the red ocean you are in); assessing defensibility of an existing position -&gt; strat/moat-analysis. ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/strategy/skills/blue-ocean) · ★16)
- **strat/bmc**: Business Model Canvas (Osterwalder): maps a business across the 9 blocks with a value proposition deep dive, delivering a completed BMC with VPC overlay and revenue model analysis. TRIGGER: "business model canvas", "BMC", "modelo de negócio", "desenha o canvas", "value proposition canvas", "revenue model", "/strat bmc". SKIP: testing whether an idea has real demand -&gt; saas/validate-idea (runs validation experiments, not canvas mapping); defining how to stand against competitors -&gt; strat/position. ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/strategy/skills/bmc) · ★16)
- **strat/extract-data**: Navigates a web page via browser integration and extracts structured data (tables, lists, prices, product listings) into CSV, markdown, or JSON, handling JS-rendered content and pagination, with WebFetch fallback for static HTML. TRIGGER: "extrai os dados desta página", "scrape this page", "extract the table", "apanha os preços do site", "export listings to CSV", "/strat extract-data &lt;url&gt;". SKIP: the ask is competitor insight rather than raw data -&gt; marketing/competitor-analysis or kb/competitive-intel (they interpret; this skill only extracts). ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/strategy/skills/extract-data) · ★16)
- **strat/five-forces**: Porter's Five Forces industry analysis: rates rivalry, threat of new entrants, supplier power, buyer power, and substitutes to produce an industry attractiveness verdict with strategic implications, optionally grounded in live competitor browsing. TRIGGER: "five forces", "cinco forças de Porter", "análise da indústria", "industry attractiveness", "atratividade do setor", "/strat five-forces". SKIP: deep dive on one competitor -&gt; marketing/competitor-analysis (company-level, not industry-level); escaping competition into new market space -&gt; strat/blue-ocean. - **strat/growth-strategy**: Strategic growth-vector selection (Ansoff Matrix + Zook adjacencies + Greiner phases): decides WHERE to grow next with risk-adjusted feasibility and a 12-month roadmap with decision gates. TRIGGER: "estratégia de crescimento", "para onde crescer", "entrar num novo mercado", "diversificar", "growth strategy", "growth vector", "expand to a new market/segment/geo", "adjacency", "Ansoff", "moat", "defensibilidade", positioning-horizon questions about the business. SKIP: the vector is already chosen and the ask is the execution roadmap -&gt; saas/growth-plan (SaaS product, stage metrics) or community… ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/strategy/skills/growth-strategy) · ★16)

- **strata:archive**: Move merged branches' pr-context dirs into archive/. User-only — run when the user explicitly asks to "clean up old branches", "archive merged work", or "tidy the vault". Never deletes, only moves, but it mutates the vault and changes what recall returns, so it does not auto-invoke (matching forget / export-to-repo / promote-to-pr). Detects merged branches via `gh pr list --state merged` or `git branch --merged main` fallback. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/archive) · ★5)
- **strata:archive-planning**: Retire a `.planning/&lt;initiative&gt;/` subdir after its content has been migrated to the vault via `/strata:bootstrap`. Runs `git mv .planning/&lt;initiative&gt; .attic/&lt;initiative&gt;` and commits. Refuses to archive subdirs that aren't fully bootstrap-processed (to avoid losing knowledge). NEVER auto-invokes, always require explicit user request, since the operation rewrites the working tree. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/archive-planning) · ★5)
- **strata:audit-config**: Check the project's Claude Code config (CLAUDE.md, .claude/settings.json, skills, agents) for staleness. Read-only. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/audit-config) · ★5)
- **strata:bootstrap**: One-time onboarding pass to seed the Strata vault from an existing codebase's docs. Scans CLAUDE.md, docs/, .planning/, and similar for candidates, then dispatches each one to a `strata:bootstrap-worker` subagent in parallel batches, the worker reads its file, classifies, writes the right kind of vault note, and returns one line. Idempotent, tracks processed files by SHA256. Use this once when first installing Strata on a repo with substantial existing documentation. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/bootstrap) · ★5)
- **strata:consolidate**: Promote durable notes out of aging per-branch pr-context dirs into the branch-agnostic lessons/ scope (rule-based, no LLM). Use when the user says "clean up old branch notes", "graduate these to lessons", "tidy pr-context", or after a wave of merged PRs. Dry-run by default; --apply to perform. Command-only — run it explicitly as /strata:consolidate. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/consolidate) · ★5)
- **strata:correct**: Edit, retire, or learn-from a vault note. Invoke autonomously when the user says "X note is wrong", "fix the part about Y", "update the status of &lt;note&gt;", "that's no longer true", "stop using &lt;note&gt;", "deprecate &lt;note&gt;" — OR corrects something Claude proposed ("no, X is actually Y", "that's wrong because Z", "we don't do it that way", "you got it backwards"). Finds the related note and applies the fix with conversation provenance (correction_source); if the corrected fact isn't in the vault yet, creates the right note instead. For ADR retirement prefer /strata:decide --supersedes; for deletio… ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/correct) · ★5)
- **strata:dashboard**: Show the current state of the Strata vault, scope counts, recent activity, drifted notes, stale ADRs, hot files, ADR↔commit linkage, suggested actions. Auto-invokable when the user says "show me the dashboard", "vault state", "what does strata see right now", "what's in the vault", or "give me a summary". Emits the same markdown that lives at `&lt;vault&gt;/&lt;repo&gt;/INDEX.md` (auto-regenerated on every write). No web server, no port, the dashboard IS a synced markdown file. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/dashboard) · ★5)
- **strata:decide**: Create a Markdown ADR in the vault's decisions/ scope. Invoke this autonomously the moment a non-trivial choice is made, a trade-off that locks something in, a constraint discovered the hard way, a choice between two reasonable options where future readers will wonder why. Don't wait for the user to ask. Pair with --supersedes when replacing an earlier decision. Skip for routine implementation choices fully explained by the code itself. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/decide) · ★5)
- **strata:doctor**: Health check for the Strata install. Verifies runtime packages, vault directory, repo namespace, search index, semantic search, MCP server, and git awareness — one glanceable checklist with a fix hint on every failure. Auto-invoke when the user says "is strata working", "did the install work", "strata seems broken", "is the vault set up", "health check", "why isn't recall finding anything", or "check my strata setup". Read-only — touches no notes, writes nothing. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/doctor) · ★5)
- **strata:domain**: Add or update a domain note in the vault's domain/ scope, vocabulary, invariants, conventions. - **strata:export-to-repo**: Promote a vault file (typically an ADR) into the host repo for git-blameable audit history. Lints with --strict before copy; never auto-commits, user runs git add / git commit themselves. Two-step with mandatory --dry-run for visibility. - **strata:find**: Grep-style line-numbered text search across the vault — the literal-string fallback to the `recall` MCP tool. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/find) · ★5)
- **strata:forget**: Move a vault file to .trash/ + JSONL audit-log entry (who/when/why/SHA256). Recoverable, not destructive. Use when the user asks to "forget", "delete", "remove", "retract", "erase", "GDPR delete", "redact" a memory file, or when PHI/secrets accidentally landed in a note. Mandatory --reason argument captured in audit log. NEVER auto-invoke, always require explicit user request; the audit trail must reflect the user's intent. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/forget) · ★5)
- **strata:graphify**: Rebuild the code map so functions/classes show up in Obsidian's graph alongside decisions and domain notes. - **strata:init**: One-time setup. Creates the vault namespace, then guides the user through the two optional add-ons that unlock the differentiated features. Installing Graphify (for code-graph awareness) and running bootstrap (to migrate existing planning docs). Interactive. Invoke when the user says "set up strata", "initialise the vault", "first install", or when a session starts in a repo where `&lt;vault&gt;/&lt;repo&gt;/` is empty. - **strata:observe**: Auto-capture a grounded, low-stakes OBSERVATION into the staging lane (status:auto, quarantined from recall until reviewed). - **strata:procedure**: Capture a procedural note, a recipe, workflow, checklist, or "how we do things here" template. - **strata:propose**: Capture an OPEN question or contested position that hasn't settled, tracking it open → contested → converging → settled. Invoke autonomously when the user says "we don't know yet whether X", "open question is Y", "we're split on Z", "I'm proposing X but not sure", "let's track this debate".
